- **ca-brainstormer**: Internal agent used by arc-investigate. Generates 5-6 evidence-based theories about problem causes after conducting investigative research. ([burnsbert/claude-arcanum](https://github.com/burnsbert/claude-arcanum/blob/HEAD/agents/ca-brainstormer.md) · ★1)

([burnsbert/claude-arcanum](https://github.com/burnsbert/claude-arcanum/blob/HEAD/agents/ca-code-review-validator.md) · ★1)
- **ca-competitor-researcher**: 競合プロファイリング・情報収集の専門家。 直接競合・間接競合・消えた競合を特定し、各社のプロファイルを作成する。 competitive-analysis チームの一員として起動される。 ([sean-sunagaku/claude-code-plugin](https://github.com/sean-sunagaku/claude-code-plugin/blob/HEAD/product/competitive-analysis/agents/ca-competitor-researcher.md) · ★38)
- **ca-data-critic**: ライブモデレーター + データ検証ゲートキーパーの専門家。 Phase 1/2 のディスカッションをモデレートし（矛盾検出・議論促進・収束判定）、 Gate 1/2 で全員参加の合意形成をファシリテートする。 Gate 2 では Devil's Advocate ラウンドと Independent Validation を運営。 確証バイアスを排除するため「望ましい結論」を知らない状態で検証する。 competitive-analysis チームの一員として起動される。 ([sean-sunagaku/claude-code-plugin](https://github.com/sean-sunagaku/claude-code-plugin/blob/HEAD/product/competitive-analysis/agents/ca-data-critic.md) · ★38)
- **ca-debugger**: Runs a scientific-method debugging session, maintaining .code_assist/.debug/DEBUG.md across the investigation. Reproduces, hypothesizes, tests to a proven root cause, then writes a regression test before the fix. Spawned by /code_assist:debug. Enforces the Iron Law - no fix without a reproduced root cause. ([prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack](https://github.com/prajwalmahajan101/claude-skills-pack/blob/HEAD/skills/code_assist/agents/ca-debugger.md) · ★2)
- **ca-feature-benchmarker**: 機能比較・価格ベンチマークの専門家。 競合アプリの機能を網羅的にリストアップし、比較マトリクスと価格テーブルを作成する。 competitive-analysis チームの一員として起動される。 ([sean-sunagaku/claude-code-plugin](https://github.com/sean-sunagaku/claude-code-plugin/blob/HEAD/product/competitive-analysis/agents/ca-feature-benchmarker.md) · ★38)
- **ca-maestro-batch-validator**: Batch validates all difficulty ≤3 tasks at once after development completes. Returns per-task COMPLETE/INCOMPLETE verdicts. - **ca-maestro-code-review**: Performs comprehensive two-pass code review (review then vet) for Maestro pipeline. Reads diary for implementation context. Opus-powered for thorough analysis. ([burnsbert/claude-arcanum](https://github.com/burnsbert/claude-arcanum/blob/HEAD/agents/ca-maestro-code-review.md) · ★1)
- **ca-maestro-code-review-responder**: Acts on vetted code review concerns - fixes bugs with regression tests, processes non-bug concerns via decision tree, documents decisions, runs final verification, and issues completion report. - **ca-maestro-dev-doer**: Standard implementer for difficulty 4-6 tasks in Maestro pipeline. Follows TDD when required, uses scout research patterns, runs tests independently, writes to diary. - **campaign-writer**: Writes and maintains the scientific log of one campaign, campaigns/&lt;run_dir_basename&gt;.md, from briefs the thinker sends. A decision log, terse by design — one line per fact, about 1500 words at most, state not chronology, results by pointer. Never runs anything and never invents a number. Use whenever a decision settles or a result has been read; not for the step ledger (tracker) and not for shortening a log that is already long (campaign-condenser). ([jlmoraleshellin/ant-comp](https://github.com/jlmoraleshellin/ant-comp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/campaign-writer.md) · ★1)
